Blocked runs emit a structured `development_artifacts_incomplete` result and finish the AG-UI lifecycle without marking development failed.
- Frontend `developmentArtifacts.ts`, `context/WorkbenchPhaseContext.tsx` and `context/workbenchPhaseState.ts` own shared phase access and live gate consumption. `ApplicationOutline/DevelopmentStatusDot` and `ApiOutlineGroup` render initial status and unfiltered counts; `WorkbenchTopBar` and historical `TestPhaseConfirmationCard` consume the same application lifecycle. Test, review, and acceptance views remain blocked while the application-wide gate is closed, including previously reached views. Test browsing creates no session; actual confirmation changes phase only after server execution registration, and an unaccepted test session is discarded.
- `AiChatPanel/workflowDebugResume.ts` binds downstream node debugging to the most recent Build thread in the current conversation that is still registered in lifecycle. Both send-button debugging and paused-plan debugging resume the execution, never the chat thread. An explicitly selected debug node may atomically replace an `awaiting_user` execution and skip its pending interaction; ordinary resume cannot, and `running`/`stopping` executions remain protected. Server checkpoint validation remains authoritative; a newer failed Build cannot fall back to an earlier success. Regression coverage is in `Frontend/tests/workflowPreview.test.ts` and `Backend/tests/test_execution_resource_locks.py`.
- 成功 End 后的显式节点调试新建 Workflow Run，不提交控制回执或已结束执行的 `resumeExecutionRunId`，也不提交旧 `resumeState`；`workflowDebugResume.ts` 按 End 回执的精确 `targetRunId` 选择原业务 Graph thread；当前会话的连续入场前拒绝不会覆盖该 End 边界，遇到已入场节点事件或仍登记的执行便停止回溯。调试续接令牌只来自当前 lifecycle 按 Run/thread 精确匹配的执行，不能使用消息 Run ID 或其他页面/应用执行；显式新调试清理旧恢复错误。暂停/失败/待确认执行的调试续接仍保留原令牌与后端校验。回归：`pnpm test:connection-recovery`（真实 End → integration_test 调试请求）、`pnpm test:workflow-preview`。
- `AiChatPanel/components/NextDevelopmentGuide` owns the post-completion/End guide and full page/API picker; `pickerTasks.ts` jointly filters status/type/search and suppresses the guide only after a successful nonempty catalog read with every page/Endpoint completed. Loading, catalog errors and unknown progress are not completion. Existing test confirmation keeps sole ownership of the all-completed next step.
- Regression entry points: Backend `tests.test_development_artifacts`, `tests.test_workflow_test_phase_confirmation`, lifecycle/continuation stream tests; Frontend `pnpm test:development-artifacts`, existing workflow/stage tests, targeted ESLint and `pnpm build`. Runtime UI verification must use Electron.
- `ApplicationOutline/DevelopmentStatusDot.less` owns the purple in-progress pulse (with reduced-motion support); `styles/global.less` defines dedicated light/dark completion greens. `WorkflowRunCard/TestPhaseConfirmationCard.less` owns the themed confirmation layout: blocked cards list each remaining artifact with its type and count, while test target and action appear only after the live gate allows entry.
- `Frontend/src/renderer/src/workbenchPhaseNavigation.ts` owns the shared phase order and per-application furthest-reached browsing record (`devagentstudio:workbench-reached-phase:<applicationId>`). `WorkbenchPhaseContext` keeps this separate from the current view and active execution; `useChatSessions` restores browsing reach from the current nonempty stage-session directory after its stale-load check. `WorkbenchTopBar` uses this record for revisiting stages, while test entry still obeys the live server gate. Application deletion clears the record. Regression coverage lives in `Frontend/tests/workbenchPhaseNavigation.test.tsx`, included by `pnpm test:development-artifacts`.

```text
Backend/app/
├── main.py
├── config.py
├── graph/          # LangGraph workflow lifecycle, state, nodes, and subgraphs
├── agents/         # First-class Deep Agent runtimes plus the read-only change-impact analyzer
├── domain/         # Framework-independent core data models and workflow contracts
├── services/       # Deterministic business rules, normalization, scheduling, and quality gates
├── tools/          # Controlled tools exposed to Deep Agents
├── protocols/      # Protocol integration
├── workspace/      # Project workspace documents and sandboxed local file/tool access
├── middleware/     # Cross-cutting Deep Agent controls
├── persistence/    # Business and runtime persistence
├── observability/  # Runtime events and observability
└── utils/          # Shared dependency-light parsing and utility helpers
```

| Path                                                    | Responsibility                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Read first / common edits                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Backend/app/main.py`                                   | FastAPI app setup and route registration for `/health`, primary `/workflow/run`, Backend-owned recovery `/execution-recovery/execute`, optionally page/API-targeted `/conversation/run`, independent read-only `/temporary-chat/run`, standalone product AG-UI actions, Electron-only deletion preparation `/application-deletion/prepare`, the dedicated data-source router, controlled `/tools/*` endpoints (including database metadata), approvals, git/workspace tools, and independent AG-UI preview controls `/preview-runtime/run`. Its lifespan clears main, creation-planning, and conversation Graph caches before closing shared checkpoint storage. | Add or change shared HTTP endpoints here; data-source HTTP changes belong in `app/routes/data_sources.py`. |
| `Backend/app/config.py`                                 | Environment-backed model provider, endpoint, credentials, and runtime settings, including `BUILD_TASK_PLAN_MAX_RETRIES` (default `2`) for platform-owned Build DAG candidate regeneration and `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_DAG_BUSINESS_SELF_CHECK_ENABLED` (default `false`) for Build-stage business self-check execution.                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Read when changing `.env` variables, model/client configuration, DAG generation retry policy, or Build business self-check behavior.                                                                                                                    |
| `Backend/app/services/workspace_bootstrap/`, `Backend/app/services/template_state.py`, `Backend/app/services/template_reconcile/`, `project_launcher.py` | First Generate keeps the confirmed TechnicalPlan and V3 project ZIP. Existing formal business revisions and the separate configuration capability path both use V4 `/v1/update` Capability Ensure: it compares canonical `application.json` Desired with `template-state.json` Applied, returns local `NO_CHANGE` without an Engine Client when equal, and uses capability-only RequestedConfig, stateSequence, size-only payloads and no SHA/digest only when a capability is missing or mismatched. Legacy `managedConfiguration` is read but excluded from V4 request and next State. `ApplicationParameterUpdater` owns `.env` and YAML parameters instead of the Engine Update. Bootstrap still accepts only the specified project roots and `.devagentstudio/template-contracts/` from Generate. | Read before changing Template Engine configuration, Bootstrap/update contracts, V3/V4 State, first-workspace initialization, or generated application parameters. |
| `Backend/app/services/application_config_orchestrator.py`, `application_parameter_updater/`, `protocols/application_config_operation.py`, `services/application_config_prerequisite.py` | Independent `/application-config/run` AG-UI preview/apply/get/resume contract merges only named configuration fields with canonical application.json, checks configRevision and installed capabilities, persists non-sensitive operation status, calls V4 only for missing capabilities, and updates exact dotenv/YAML leaves. `application_config_prerequisite` deterministically recognizes application-level theme/icon/name/login/authorization intent before direct code classification; same-value pure configuration completes without an Agent, while mixed requests preserve the original request and classify only the remaining business intent. Parameter writes use a `0600` short-lived before/after journal and recover safely after interruption. Direct conversation pauses at an explicit configuration card, then resumes the same checkpoint only after its request-bound operation is applied. Formal Revision has no configuration Delta path. The settings and conversation clients use this route; the Electron write IPC rejects bypasses for generated applications' managed config fields. | Read with `services/application_config/`, `template_reconcile/`, frontend `service/applicationConfigOperation.ts`, `SettingsPage/`, `WorkflowRunCard/`, and Electron `main/index.ts` when changing config transaction, field mapping, or failure recovery. |
| `Backend/packaging/devagentstudio-backend.spec`             | PyInstaller onedir definition that bundles the Backend application, built-in skills under `_internal/app/builtin_skills`, frontend page-template source under `_internal/app/page_templates`, package data, and hidden imports; the optional built-in grammar profile excludes custom-only Tree-sitter bindings. | Read when changing packaged Python/data dependencies, grammar support, or frozen resource layout. |
| `Backend/packaging/verify_bundled_skills.py`            | Cross-platform post-PyInstaller validator used by both Backend staging scripts to reject bundles missing built-in skills or page templates, and to compare packaged templates with frontend source. | Update with required skill/template files or supported PyInstaller resource layouts. |
| `Backend/packaging/backend_server.py`                   | Frozen Backend entry point: loads packaged configuration, validates required built-in skills before Uvicorn starts, and launches the API server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Read when changing packaged Backend startup, frozen resource discovery, or fail-fast requirements.                                                                                                                  |
| `Backend/app/graph/workflow.py`                         | Primary LangGraph workflow declaration: page/API development enters `api_design_readiness_gate`, which checks current Endpoint API Design versions before `inspect_workspace -> prepare_build_tasks`. Production task planning uses the async Planning adapter and stops at a PendingPlan confirmation gate; exact Confirm promotes Formal and resumes Build, Regenerate consumes the old Pending and creates a new PlanningRun, and Abandon ends the matching execution. Legacy EntitySourceBinding nodes remain registered only as an independent flow. Later phases retain explicit test/review/acceptance gates and failures route to `handle_failure`. | Read when adding, removing, renaming, rerouting workflow phases, changing workflow checkpoint behavior, or selecting planning authority. Keep node logic in `graph/nodes/` or subgraphs. |
| `Backend/app/graph/direct_modification_workflow.py` | Independent optionally targeted conversation Graph: bounded read-only workspace scan and Q&A, intent classification, direct local implementation fixes owned by frontend/backend/fullstack, optional pre-execution `implementation_fix_confirmation` controlled by `confirmScopeBeforeExecution` (default on), pre-execution rejection for paths outside the current owner's writable directory, confirmed SmallTask scope expansions, or formal Workflow handoff. Requests without a concrete desired change enter clarification even when scope confirmation is off. No separate workspace-write Agent/node exists. Backend-first fullstack execution is followed by `validate_direct_fix`: affected frontend changes run mandatory tsc/Vite checks before the existing project restart validation, without unit tests. Successful changes finalize without entering formal acceptance; the bounded repair loop returns to the same validation. Checkpoints use the StageSession thread; `services/conversation_execution.py` connects scope `conversation` to Workbench lifecycle, DurableExecution, leases and shared recovery. | Read with `graph/nodes/direct_modification.py`, `services/direct_validation.py`, `graph/nodes/direct_repair.py`, `agents/direct_modification.py`, and `protocols/direct_modification.py`. |
| `Backend/app/graph/nodes/direct_repair.py` | Dedicated conversation repair node: gives a read-only RepairPlanner exact diff paths plus verified frontend diagnostic source paths, executes bounded shared SmallTask tasks with the current plan strategy, persists the plan, and returns successful repairs to `validate_direct_fix`. Missing or placeholder paths fail with evidence; explicit formal revision escalation keeps its confirmation gate. | Read with `graph/direct_modification_workflow.py`, `services/direct_validation.py`, `services/small_task.py`, `agents/repair_planner/`, and `protocols/direct_modification_projection.py`. |
| `Backend/app/graph/state.py`                            | `ProjectState` TypedDict for compact Graph State: project/request/workspace metadata, selected targets, EntitySourceBinding submission, formal artifact paths, and requirement-revision transaction identity. Application configuration prerequisites are held by the direct conversation checkpoint and its request-bound operation; RequirementSpec keeps only business authorization clarification. It also holds checkpoint-internal TechnicalPlan repair candidate/errors, runtime `project_plan` context, Build DAG confirmation/progress, explicit `resume_execution_run_id` for DAG Recovery source selection, `test_phase_confirmation`, stable `test_target`, build/test summaries plus separate Markdown/internal-JSON test-report paths, unit-test generation context/mapping/change sets, quality gate, SmallTask state, code-review scan/repair/build state, `acceptance_phase_confirmation`, launch/acceptance evidence, failures, and timeline. Internal TechnicalPlan repair fields are excluded from AG-UI projection and never become formal artifacts; `project_plan` is a runtime projection for the current Build scope. Workspace `build-task-plan.json` remains the DAG confirmation authority. Build-gate/DAG-generation retry returns to `prepare_build_tasks`; ordinary task retry resumes `build`; completed Build pauses at `test_phase_confirmation` before `integration_test`, and clean review pauses at `acceptance_phase_confirmation` before the acceptance subgraph. | Read when changing workflow state keys, persisted file references, readiness/binding resume contracts, DAG/test/acceptance confirmation, code-review repair recovery, stale-checkpoint recovery, node contracts, or planning artifacts. |
| `Backend/app/graph/nodes/`                              | Thin phase nodes for classification, requirements, planning/detail confirmation, task preparation, direct modification, build/testing wrappers, and lifecycle phases. Task preparation checks confirmed formal artifacts, template readiness, scope detail, and API consistency before compiling a seven-stage DAG-generation snapshot through the shared progress service. Platform-owned DAG validation failures route to bounded model regeneration or `handle_failure`; they are not exposed as user task-splitting decisions. | Read the file matching the phase before changing node behavior; keep deterministic routing/state updates here and delegate reusable rules to `services/`.                                                           |
| `Backend/app/graph/nodes/tasks.py` | Shared formal-artifact, Endpoint API Design readiness, and scope projections for the current async Unit planner. Production task generation and confirmation are owned by `task_planning_adapter.py`; the removed Scope-level generator and replacement merge are not restored. | Read with `Backend/app/graph/nodes/task_planning_adapter.py` before changing planning inputs or readiness gates. |
| `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/build.py`                  | Build Subgraph behind `build`: at Build Run start it reads the current confirmed workspace `build-task-plan.json`, requires `build-dag.v4`, `status=ready`, a matching frozen `template_context`, `task_graph.validation.is_valid=true`, and matching Build scope, then binds a read-only per-run copy by canonical SHA-256. Before every leaf dispatch it checks that the planning authority still has that digest; drift terminates the Run rather than switching plans. After all Build tasks succeed, it replays the confirmed DAG's platform Route/authorization/AuthConstants projections, records separate `platform_projection_evidence`, and only then performs read-only EDD. It dispatches each ready task through an independent bounded Agent invocation, combines the task's authorized snapshot paths with that invocation's recorded write/delete tool paths, and checks scope only against the resulting task-owned change set so concurrent tasks cannot inherit each other's violations. Engineering acceptance always runs; business acceptance runs independently only when `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_DAG_BUSINESS_SELF_CHECK_ENABLED=true`, otherwise it records an explicit skipped status before the aggregated Repair decision. Database tasks continue to use database execution evidence. FAILED re-entry preserves completed tasks and retries local failures or prepared repairs; INTERRUPTED/explicit Build restart creates a fresh Build Run. Both refresh workspace facts; repair-scope/database-approval continuations remain bounded. | Read when changing task dispatch, concurrency, task-owned file attribution, Build Run plan binding, platform projection timing, acceptance aggregation, confirmation recovery, owner result collection, explicit retry recovery, scheduler coordination after code generation, database approval pauses, or repair planning. |
| `Backend/app/domain/api_design.py` | Current-only Pydantic contract for confirmed `endpoint-field-mapping.v7` documents. `api_design_fields.py`, `api_design_values.py`, and `api_design_database.py` split shared fields, value rules, and single-table query/write contracts. `sourceBinding` preserves the selected object; `externalApiBindings` owns upstream request targets; `value_mapping` owns business/fixed/context outputs. See `docs/FIELD_VALUE_RULES.md`. | Read before changing Endpoint field mappings, database writes, query trees, CRUD rules, or source snapshots. |
| `Backend/app/services/api_design.py`                    | Deterministic Endpoint API-design service: creates immutable Endpoint field snapshots, enumerates real-time source candidates, validates CRUD partitioning, write-value and query types, complete `fieldMappings`/`databaseWrites`, redacted source snapshots, and current design readiness. TechnicalPlan global entity semantics remain outside Endpoint↔Source mappings. | Read with the domain model, data-source catalog service, Endpoint document store, and node before changing design behavior. |
| `Backend/app/services/api_design_mapping_rules.py` | Current v7 mapping rules for response-read/external source identities and processing descriptions; database write targets are validated through `databaseWrites`, while query conditions use the database query tree. | Read with the domain and API design service when changing mapping cardinality or source identity. |
| `Backend/app/workspace/endpoint_design_documents.py`   | Reads and atomically writes `.devagentstudio/plans/endpoints/endpoint--<contractId>--<endpointId>.json/.md`, computes the TechnicalPlan contract fingerprint, and requires the confirmed dual-file pair for readiness. | Read before changing Endpoint artifact naming, fingerprinting, Markdown rendering, or persistence guarantees. |
| `Backend/app/graph/nodes/api_design.py`                 | Implements deterministic `api_design_readiness_gate`; independent mapping save writes Endpoint artifacts before this gate. Page readiness checks every `requiredEndpointId`, Endpoint readiness checks only the selected Contract/Endpoint, and confirmation rechecks the complete displayed revision set before entering `inspect_workspace`. | Read with workflow request/lifecycle/projection/runtime adapters and the frontend API-design panel. |
| `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/testing.py`                | Testing Subgraph behind `integration_test`: runs dependency installation, frontend/backend Build checks, pauses through an AG-UI confirmation for the optional advisory frontend performance test, launches the user `frontend` dev server with `frontend_performance_runner`, records the non-blocking Lighthouse check, and performs the integration quality gate. Every quality-gate pass overwrites the internal JSON and user-facing Markdown test reports so repair retries always publish the latest result; it no longer calls TestGenerationAgent or executes frontend/backend unit tests. Any blocking integration check routes to the independent `small_task_repair` loop; API contracts are validated before build and repair plans never route directly to `build`. | Read when changing integration build/performance confirmation, quality gate rules, test evidence shape (including `performanceScores`/`performanceMetrics`), test-report refresh, revision requests, integration repair budgets, repair scope, or repair-task routing. |

| `Backend/app/services/repository_branch.py`, `Backend/app/services/git_branch.py`, `Backend/app/services/git_credentials.py`, `Backend/app/services/git_identity.py` | Own the application's remote-repository branch. `git_branch.py` holds the Git ref-name rules (shared by branch actions and commit-push; it depends on nothing, because both callers importing each other would be a cycle) and `RepositoryBranchError`'s successor `GitBranchError`. `repository_branch.py` owns `git ls-remote` existence checks, `create_local_branch` (checkout -b + push, used when an iteration starts on a new branch), `delete_remote_branch`, and `push_baseline_to_branch`. **The workspace's local branch must always match the application branch name**: historical preview (`git rev-parse <branch>`) and read-only source (`git show <branch>:<path>`) both resolve the branch locally, so `push_baseline_to_branch` calls `_ensure_local_branch` first — `git push HEAD:refs/heads/<branch>` only creates the branch on the *remote*, leaving the local repo on the `git init` default and making the application's first branch permanently unviewable; every action returns a `{branchName, status, commitSha, message}` result and **never raises**. `read_workspace_repository_target` reads `repoUrl`/`branchName`/`branchOverwriteConfirmed` from the workspace `application.json`; an empty branch name means the application has no configured branch and every caller skips. Only a user-confirmed overwrite force-pushes, so a divergent remote branch is refused rather than silently destroyed. `git_credentials.py` owns the credential source for every remote push: `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_GIT_CREDENTIAL_SOURCE=env` (default, outside the company) injects `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_GIT_USERNAME`/`DEVAGENTSTUDIO_GIT_TOKEN` into the URL, while `helper` (inside the company) passes the bare URL so the local Git credential helper (Windows Credential Manager / macOS Keychain) authenticates and the server validates the employee's own repository permission. It also supplies `git_env()` (`GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, because the Electron-launched backend has no TTY) and classifies authentication failures into mode-specific, actionable messages. `git_identity.py` owns the commit author identity: the company Gitee validates the author email (`git002 Unauthorized email account cannot submit code`), so the baseline commit uses `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_GIT_AUTHOR_NAME`/`_EMAIL` when both are set, else the machine's global `git config user.email`, else a placeholder that at least lets Bootstrap finish. The template baseline commit message is fixed to the company board-number format in `workspace_bootstrap/git_manager.py` (`_TEMPLATE_BASELINE_COMMIT_MESSAGE`). | Read with `protocols/repository_branch.py`, `services/version_publish.py` (`build_authenticated_remote_url` is the single credential-injection point and defers to `services/git_credentials.py`), `services/revision_preview.py`, and `services/workspace_bootstrap/service.py` before changing branch rules, remote push behavior, the credential source, or the commit identity. |

It derives JSON Schema/API bindings and MySQL target tables without exposing credentials; external API Build summaries project shared connection plus only the current page/Endpoint-linked operations, replacing complete samples with bounded `request_shape`/`response_shape`, preserving response semantics and mappings, and deriving `mapped_entity_path` from the mappings' common array prefix. | Read before changing entity normalization, schema derivation, table mapping, field validation, or external API build context. |

| `Backend/app/workspace/code_changes.py`                 | Workspace snapshot diff capture for DeepAgent file changes, producing frontend-compatible `WorkspaceCodeChangeSet` payloads with workspace-root name, normalized relative file paths, and code-only changed diff lines while skipping ignored dirs, sensitive files, symlinks, and binary text leakage.                                                                                              | Read when changing workflow code-change capture, workspace path metadata, diff truncation, or AG-UI `codeChanges` output.                                                                                          |
| `Backend/app/workspace/run_lease.py`                    | Process-local activity registry used by `/workflow/run` to track and clean up active Graph runs without rejecting overlapping workspaces, pages, or resource claims.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Read when changing workflow concurrency, active-run cleanup, or reintroducing an explicit concurrency policy.                                                                                                      |
| `Backend/app/workspace/virtual_paths.py`                | Shared virtual-workspace path instructions plus helpers that recognize host `workspaceRoot` aliases accidentally supplied as DeepAgent virtual paths.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Read when changing the model-visible `/` root contract or repeated-host-path safeguards.                                                                                                                            |
| `Backend/app/workspace/spec_documents.py`               | Writes product-facing RequirementSpec Markdown/internal JSON, including display-only entity information while omitting technical data-source and engineering-acceptance sections; detects Markdown edits before confirmation. | Read when changing RequirementSpec persistence, entity rendering, synchronization, or artifact roots. |
| `Backend/app/workspace/plan_documents.py`               | Writes current TechnicalPlan Markdown/JSON and synchronizes developer edits; renders three-part architecture, API/schema/entity semantics, and page references. Legacy EntitySourceBinding Markdown rendering remains for the independent old entry; current Endpoint API-design Markdown belongs to `endpoint_design_documents.py`. | Read with `product_plan_documents.py` and `endpoint_design_documents.py` when changing formal planning persistence. |
| `Backend/app/workspace/detail_design_documents.py`      | Persists only EntitySourceBinding JSON/Markdown under `.devagentstudio/plans/entities/` and stores `source_binding` references in TechnicalPlan. No page/API artifact is written or hydrated. External API bindings use the normalized `api_info`/`response_handling`/`field_mappings` contract. This retained legacy artifact is not read into the current DAG Planning context. | Read when changing legacy entity-binding artifact names, references, hydration, or external API contract projection. |
| `Backend/app/services/build_task_plan_lifecycle.py`, `Backend/app/services/dag_planning_regeneration.py`, `Backend/app/services/planning_recovery_contracts.py`, `Backend/app/services/build_task_planning_service.py`, `Backend/app/workspace/task_documents.py`, `Backend/app/workspace/planning_recovery_documents.py` | Own distinct Formal `.devagentstudio/plans/build-task-plan.json`, Pending `.devagentstudio/drafts/plans/build-task-plan.pending.json`, Recovery `.devagentstudio/runtime/planning-recovery/<source_workflow_run_id>.json`, and Regenerate operation facts `.devagentstudio/runtime/dag-regeneration/<old_draft_digest>.json`. The operation fact binds the exact old DraftIdentity, thread, current execution Run, consumption phase, and generated PlanningRun so Native Recovery can resume after the old Pending is consumed without treating an absent Pending as authorization. Scope Assembly computes stable `review_task_ids`, `platform_task_ids`, `new_task_ids`, and `reused_task_ids`; Pending persistence validates `planning_provenance.schema_version=planning-provenance.v2` before canonical SHA-256 signing. Recovery stores only the final failed source Run's current ready Candidate bodies after `UNIT_GENERATION_INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE`; it is not PlanningRun/Pending/Formal authority. The exact Native Recovery source may supply Candidates to either ordinary generation or an already-consumed Regenerate attempt; both recheck current inputs and fail open to full generation if Snapshot is unavailable. Recovered Candidates receive a new identity and source provenance without a model Attempt. The Pending writer binds backend metadata, rejects caller identity/failed validation, and writes atomically without touching Formal. Confirm checks exact request identity, self digest, current Formal baseline, fresh inputs, Scope and DAG gate, then atomically replaces Formal after removing both `draft_identity` and `planning_provenance`, before deleting matching Pending. Route Projection is not a DAG task or persisted route baseline: after a successful (including empty application-scope) DAG, `graph/subgraphs/build.py` invokes the v2 template contract and records only captured Workspace changes in Build evidence. | Read `docs/BUILD_TASK_PLAN_CONFIRM_PROMOTION.md`, `docs/DAG_TASK_GENERATION_UNIT_CANDIDATE_PLAN.md`, `docs/SEQUENTIAL_DAG_PLANNING.md`, `docs/template/route-projection-post-dag-finalization-plan.md`, `Backend/tests/test_planning_recovery.py`, `test_build_task_plan_lifecycle.py`, `test_build_task_plan_regenerate.py`, `test_dag_confirm_authority_cutover.py`, and `test_pending_build_task_plan_documents.py` before changing promotion, identity, persistence, Recovery, Regenerate, application-level exclusivity, Route Projection finalization, or refresh/cancellation behavior. |
| `Backend/app/workspace/workspace_snapshot_documents.py` | Helpers for reading workspace snapshot JSON and resolving the `.devagentstudio/cache` artifact root used by `inspect_workspace`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Read when changing workspace snapshot persistence paths or cache loading.                                                                                                                                           |
| `Backend/app/workspace/test_documents.py`               | Atomically writes internal quality-gate data to `.devagentstudio/reports/test-report.json` and renders the current user-facing `.devagentstudio/reports/test-report.md`. The Markdown always summarizes frontend/backend Build checks, conditionally includes bounded Lighthouse scores/metrics, and strips full logs, HTML bodies, and host absolute paths. | Read with Testing Subgraph and workflow projection when changing test report persistence, rendering, sanitization, or artifact paths. |
| `Backend/app/workspace/code_review_documents.py`        | Renders and atomically overwrites `.devagentstudio/reports/code-review.md` from the normalized scan snapshot. Diff reports include the complete server-validated reviewed-file list; full reports publish only front/back scan roots and counts. Repair actions, logs, and host absolute paths are excluded. | Read with the Code Review Subgraph and workflow projection when changing the review report contract or persistence path. |
| `Backend/app/middleware/approvals.py`                   | Approval grants, reusable operation rules, and protected-operation fingerprints.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Read when changing approval lifecycle, scopes, or risk gating.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Backend/app/persistence/run_store.py`                  | Writes `.devagentstudio/runs/<runId>` artifacts and JSONL run events.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Read when changing run artifact lifecycle, formats, or retention.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Backend/app/persistence/checkpoints.py`                | Owns workspace-scoped LangGraph SQLite checkpointers for the main workflow, including `.devagentstudio/checkpoints/checkpoints.sqlite` path creation, optional global `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_CHECKPOINT_DB` override, conservative retention cleanup, exact session-thread row deletion, target-workspace row deletion, and connection shutdown. Session deletion checks recorded workspace identity before deleting a thread from a shared database. Full-app deletion identifies rows from checkpoint channel values, metadata, and known lifecycle threads; it removes only matching rows from a shared override DB, while a local DB connection is closed before its project directory is trashed. | Read when changing workflow state persistence, checkpoint retention, recovery/deletion behavior, or `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_CHECKPOINT_DB` handling. |

| `Backend/app/observability/telemetry_models.py`, `telemetry.py` | Phase-one optional-field telemetry models and backend-only emit entry points. The caller captures occurrence time and submits validation, missing-field defaults, JSON assembly, and one-record POST to a bounded two-thread executor. Producer defaults include only OS and product version from `Frontend/package.json` (included by `Backend/packaging/devagentstudio-backend.spec`); source/environment remain caller-supplied. The temporary default user is `Hupy/test`; explicit values/nulls are preserved. There is no return value, persistent queue, or retry. The URL is read from `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_TELEMETRY_URL` by `telemetry.py::telemetry_post_url`. | Read with `docs/design/observability/telemetry-phase1-v1.jsonc` and `telemetry-usage.md` when changing field shape, business call sites, executor limits, POST behavior, or runtime configuration. |
| `Backend/app/utils/model_output.py`                     | Shared best-effort JSON object extraction for direct model outputs, including fenced or commentary-wrapped JSON.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Read when changing model JSON parsing used by requirements, product/technical planning, task preparation, or entity-source binding.                                                                                |

## Current Build DAG Contract

The current Build path is `confirmed formal artifacts + template readiness + PageImplementationContract + confirmed Endpoint API designs → api_design_readiness_gate → inspect_workspace → prepare_build_tasks → DAG confirmation → build`. The following boundaries are part of the current contract and should be checked together when changing this path:

| Concern | Current contract | Read first |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Runtime context | `project_plan` is a read-only runtime projection used to assemble the current Build context. It is not a new editable formal artifact, and DAG generation/confirmation must not write it back into TechnicalPlan Markdown or JSON. | `Backend/app/graph/nodes/tasks.py`, `Backend/app/services/build_context_resolver.py`, `Backend/app/workspace/plan_documents.py` |
| DAG prerequisites | `RequirementSpec`, `ProductPlan`, `UiManifest` (confirmed or explicitly skipped), `TechnicalPlan`, frozen `TemplateState`/`template_context`, current PageImplementationContract, Endpoint semantics, confirmed EntitySourceBinding, and API consistency must pass before candidate generation. | `Backend/app/graph/nodes/tasks.py`, `Backend/app/services/template_state.py`, `Backend/app/services/development_readiness.py`, `Backend/app/services/build_context_resolver.py` |
| Generation and validation | The platform owns Unit/task boundaries. Candidate structural normalization may fill deterministic fields and merge exact duplicates; compiled business checks also enforce one frontend implementation owner per stable Endpoint identity. It must not delete or rewrite semantic menu/route/page-boundary tasks. Violations stay in `task_graph.validation.errors`; `task_preparer.py` feeds them back for bounded automatic regeneration, and exhausted attempts become platform failure rather than a user task-splitting request. | `Backend/app/services/build_task_planner.py`, `Backend/app/agents/main/task_preparer_prompt.py`, `Backend/app/agents/main/task_preparer.py`, `Backend/app/services/build_task_menu.py` |
| Deliverable contract | `deliverables[].kind` must use the exact `DELIVERABLE_KINDS` allowlist; values outside it are rejected and do not receive a business acceptance projection. Existing `deliverables[].paths` behavior remains unchanged. | `Backend/app/agents/main/task_preparer_prompt.py`, `Backend/app/services/business_acceptance.py` |
| Artifact authority | Formal `build-task-plan.json` and Pending `build-task-plan.pending.json` are separate. Exact Pending confirmation promotes Formal. Build binds an immutable per-run copy using a planning-content SHA-256. Before dispatch, `.devagentstudio/plans/build-runs/execution-<SHA-256 of Workflow run ID>.json` binds Workflow run, thread, scope, Build Run and formal digest; each committed batch atomically updates its internal tasks/results there. A recovery child records its own execution identity and the validated inherited baseline in `progress`, with `progress_source_run_id` naming the original progress owner. `execution_stage` distinguishes `bound`, `inherited`, `prepared`, and `batch_committed`; a selected retry or ready repair persists its prepared tasks, retry counts and old results before Runner dispatch, and later batches replace this with child-owned committed progress. Recovery checks the immutable plan and task contracts and follows only the verified RecoveryAttempt parent chain to matching Build progress. For a child interrupted before preparing its retry, the exact started FAILED-origin Build RecoveryAttempt and its fork parent authorize re-preparation. Ordinary interrupted continuation restores progress without activating failed-task retry; a prepared retry continues its pending work without incrementing the retry count again. Scheduler status/evidence writeback remains hash-neutral and atomically updates matching Formal tasks under the promotion lock. Pending identity is unchanged. | `Backend/app/workspace/task_documents.py`, `Backend/app/services/build_task_plan_lifecycle.py`, `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/build.py`, `Backend/app/services/execution_recovery_executor.py` |
| Ownership boundary | Agents own real page implementation; after all tasks succeed, the platform replays the confirmed DAG to write shared routes, optional authorization resources, and AuthConstants. Bootstrap and planning do not create page placeholders, menus, routes, or branch-dependent artifacts. For real APIs, the Frontend Agent generates business API modules that reuse the template-owned `service.ts`; no generated response-adapter Task or file is part of the Build DAG. Static modules do not use HTTP transport. Normal Build does not create `database:*` Units or database-owner tasks. | `Backend/app/agents/main/task_preparer_prompt.py`, `Backend/app/agents/frontend/generator.py`, `Backend/app/graph/nodes/tasks.py`, `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/build.py` |
| User confirmation | The AG-UI `build_task_plan_confirmation` mode is read-only and exposes the identity-bound `confirm`、`abandon` and `regenerate` actions. It shows the current target, directly related Endpoints, one topologically ordered `reviewTasks` list tagged `new`/`reused`, and a status-only summary for unrelated retained tasks. Confirm resumes the original checkpoint; abandon marks the pending plan abandoned, stops the execution, and releases its locks. Frontend ownership is split: left `MessageList`/`ProcessSteps/DagGenerationProgress` keeps Unit progress and the completion navigation; right `RightPanelTabs` shows `待确认任务` only for the current PendingPlan owner and `StageOutputPanel` contains the only full confirmation form. First navigation is keyed by DraftIdentity and runs once per app/owner; explicit left navigation remains repeatable, and consumed Pending falls back from the right tab without reopening a closed panel. Platform validation/generation failures are not manually corrected. | `Backend/app/protocols/workflow/request.py`, `Backend/app/protocols/workflow/run_control.py`, `Backend/app/protocols/workflow/definition.py`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/AiChatPanel.tsx`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/dagPendingPlanPresentation.ts`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/MessageList/`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/ProcessSteps/DagGenerationProgress.tsx`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/StageOutputPanel/`, `Frontend/tests/dagGenerationProgress.test.ts`, `docs/DAG_TASK_GENERATION_AND_VALIDATION_MININAL_CHANGE_PLAN.md` |

### Merge integration: task planning and Build execution

- Current backend Unit prompts in `Backend/app/agents/main/backend_unit_task_rules.py` retain module ErrorCode/IBizErrorCode planning and BaseMapper-first persistence rules. `Backend/app/agents/field_value_prompt_rules.py` injects English field-value semantics (`field_value_prompt_rules_cn.py` is a non-runtime Chinese reference) between planning and `agents/data_source/generator.py`, with stage-specific pre-call processing, access, post-call composition, missing-value and sourceBinding-only operation rules. Configuration examples live in `docs/FIELD_MAPPING_EXAMPLES.md` and are not injected into project prompts. The deleted Scope-level TaskPreparer is not restored.
- `Backend/app/workspace/task_documents.py` retains scheduler runtime writeback to the matching Formal DAG and stores a per-Workflow-execution Build binding/progress record before dispatch and after each committed batch. Runtime status, evidence and timestamps do not change the Build planning digest; Pending draft identity and exact confirmation checks remain separate. Build Run plan snapshots remain immutable.
- Bootstrap source changes use exact `backend.bootstrap:database` / `backend.bootstrap:external_api` requirements in the new Unit planner. Existing tasks cannot hide a missing source capability. Regression: `Backend/tests/test_bootstrap_scope_replanning.py`.

## Frontend

生命周期客户端 `service/applicationLifecycle.ts` 的 Attach/Get 单次 AG-UI 状态同步请求最多等待 20 秒；超时中止客户端等待并通过原 `finally` 释放工作区共享查询，模板生成等业务动作不套用此期限。连接提示的“重新同步”在工作台和欢迎页只读取当前状态，不提交执行恢复或模板重试；预览连接同样使用独立只读 `reconnect`。`useWorkbenchRunRefresh.ts` 和手动同步按会话/请求代次丢弃迟到结果，失败不覆盖最近有效 lifecycle。回归：`pnpm test:connection-recovery` 的 `applicationLifecycleTimeout.test.ts`、`workbenchRunRefresh.test.ts`。

模板初始化的通信失败通过独立 `template_generation_failed` 前端事件保留连接错误，不再由通用 Planning `run_failed` 覆盖为 healthy；底部连接/错误兜底和欢迎页规划视图均沿用 Bootstrap 或 Template Reconcile 的专用重试。两种重试先同步后端当前资格：Bootstrap 只重试失败初始化，Template Reconcile 只提交现有 `retry_template_reconcile` 动作，不重新启动技术规划或绕过确认门。回归：`Frontend/tests/templateRecovery.test.tsx`、`Frontend/tests/applicationPlanningRuntime.test.ts`。

Current Recovery Incident ownership: `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/recoveryActionPlan.ts` strictly parses the shared Backend `recovery-action-plan.v1`; `recoveryIncident.ts` derives the single current incident for Planning and Workbench; and `components/RecoveryIncidentCard/` owns the shared current control surface, with the Planning component and Welcome path as thin wrappers. `executionRecoveryState.ts` accepts Workbench `interrupted` or `failed` candidates only when the ActionPlan is valid and identity-matched. Workbench Recovery is also scoped to lifecycle `activeExecutions`: Ended runs are removed from lifecycle GET projections, pruned from retained frontend projections on the newer lifecycle revision, and rejected by `/execution-recovery/execute`. Browser-to-Backend health is a separate dimension owned by `service/connectionState.ts` and `components/ConnectionStatusBanner/`: transport/SSE/refresh failures never synthesize or clear a Recovery Incident, and passive reconnect only refreshes durable truth. `AiChatPanel` presents one global fallback card immediately above the bottom Composer/dock: connection failure takes visual priority over a durable Recovery Incident, and an explicit Retry first refreshes Backend truth before executing the current Backend-approved action. During an active Workbench retry or execution, `globalFallbackState.ts` suppresses the previous failure card and its error; the current failure entry returns only after the new execution settles with a recoverable failure. `MessageList` never renders generic AgentErrorCard for either current or historical messages; node-specific failure states remain. `globalFallbackState.ts::latestConversationFailure` supplies the latest message failure to the bottom surface even without a Recovery Incident, and new requests or successful messages do not inherit historical errors. Code-review retry keeps its original callback in the bottom fallback; template-generation retry remains explicit. Recoverable actions execute through `/execution-recovery/execute` with Backend-issued `incidentId` and `actionId`; `needs_attention` keeps a permanent `retry_current_failure` entry that submits only the current projected `sourceRunId`, after which Backend re-resolves the canonical lineage head and current RecoveryActionPlan. The legacy `ExecutionRecoveryCard` and frontend `/execution-recovery/run` path are removed.

Workflow failure display: a `DagPlanningError` with one issue exposes that issue's `code` through the existing Workflow `summary.errorCode`. For Workbench `UNIT_GENERATION_INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE`, `MessageList` suppresses the duplicate upper history error card; `recoveryIncident.ts` shows a concise model setup/invoke failure only when the failed Workflow run ID matches the current durable Recovery source and the structured code is present. Other message errors and Planning presentation keep their existing owners. Workbench `RecoveryActionPlan.primaryAction.targetNode` carries the Backend-verified retry entry only for display; raw recovery diagnostics stay hidden from the current Workbench card. The Backend ActionPlan still owns retry authority.

Planning Runtime ownership: `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/applicationPlanningRuntime.ts` owns independent AG-UI execution, authoritative reconcile, submit/save/revision/retry/stop and temporary streaming subscriptions. Its exclusive transport primitive remains the only application-planning Graph-write owner. `applicationPlanningRecovery.ts` is the dedicated read-only AG-UI control-plane client with bounded timeout/retry and strictly parses `application-planning-recovery.v1`, including optional safe `failureDiagnostic` fields; Runtime single-flights those reads and atomically commits Workflow, lifecycle, and recovery through `reconcile_received`. Retry always reconciles first: a recoverable plan executes the Backend-issued `incidentId`/`actionId`, while `needs_attention` submits `retry_current_failure` for Backend re-resolution; neither path resends the original answer/request. `awaiting_user`, running, conflict, and unverified states remain non-retryable. Authoritative `RUN_ERROR` settles the write transport, records the failure, then performs one post-terminal reconcile so a failed Native child immediately exposes the Backend-resolved current recovery head. `applicationPlanningRecovery.ts` feeds diagnostic-only fields into the current Incident presentation; ActionPlan status and action identity remain Backend-owned. `applicationPlanningWorkflowState.ts` requires a projected Native Interrupt for current user interaction. `activeApplicationPlanning.ts` keeps business `error` separate from `syncError`, stores the recovery projection, and blocks mutations while reconciling or uncertain. Stateless interaction, checkpoint identity and bounded-stop helpers live in `applicationPlanningRuntimeHelpers.ts`, while `applicationPlanningProductConversation.ts` owns the pure product-conversation interaction/error domain functions and the ChatComposer path only re-exports them for compatibility. `hooks/useActiveApplicationPlannings.ts` owns the synchronous Canonical State getter/reducer, cold loading, visibility and template orchestration; it holds no component execution handlers. Runtime updates Canonical State before publishing recovered Workflow history. No new frontend storage contract is added.

`AppEntryPage.tsx` mounts `hooks/useApplicationPlanningRuntimes.ts` once to manage Runtime instances by application plus thread, dispose removed/replaced instances, and route Workbench submit/save/revision/retry/stop actions directly. A design-stage formal revision ensures the original planning Runtime synchronously, without a Modal-mount wait. `hooks/useApplicationPlanningWorkbenchBridge.ts` retains `deliverPlanningChunk`, current-thread history buffering, and the existing Workbench revision-continuation bridge; it is not a Planning state owner.

Only the actually visible `ApplicationPagePlanningModal.tsx` is mounted. It consumes current state, temporary streaming content, template loading and business actions; `Welcome/applicationPlanningPresentation.ts` holds its pure progress/page-count projections. `hooks/useApplicationPlanningStreamingContent.ts` subscribes the visible view to Runtime presentation content without storing tokens in Canonical State. WorkbenchPage/LeftPanel pass the `onSavePlanningRequirementSpec` business action to AiChatPanel; Runtime saves and updates current state, while the view only updates its document cache and toast from `RequirementSpecDraftSaveResult`. Focused cases A-M live in `Frontend/tests/applicationPlanningRuntime.test.ts`, with J-M covering shared pre-submit ownership, busy state, internal recovery retry and Canonical-before-submit ordering. `pnpm test:application-planning-runtime` uses the repository's Vite temporary-runner pattern. Consumer integration has not been validated because the user explicitly requested no verification.

The product and technical brand is **DevAgent Studio**. User-visible labels use the spaced name; machine identifiers use `devagentstudio`, TypeScript/Python symbols use `DevAgentStudio`, environment variables use `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_*`, and workspace artifacts use `.devagentstudio`. The Electron bridge is `window.devAgentStudio`, and the packaged backend executable is `devagentstudio-backend`.

| Path                                                                                                          | Responsibility                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Read first / common edits                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Frontend/src/main/index.ts`                                                                                  | Electron main process: app/window/auth/preview lifecycle, storage and workspace IPC, unified controlled application deletion, template clone/retry, local sessions, and planning-artifact inspection. `applications.json` is strictly an index of `{ id, workspaceRoot, name, lastOpenedAt }`; `workspace:read-application` and `workspace:write-application` respectively read and update the sole `.devagentstudio/application.json` source, with real writes advancing `configRevision`. Application deletion is independent of the current design/planning/development stage: index-only removal stays in the renderer, while the main process requires `applicationId` plus `workspaceRoot`, completes and verifies backend deletion preparation itself, then performs protected-path/project-marker checks before trashing sessions and the managed project. `sessions:create` is the only current-contract chat-session creation path: it generates the AG-UI Thread, persists workflow and phase/stage identity without artifact ownership, and allocates DESIGN/PLAN/DEVELOPMENT sequence under the main-process lock; when a consumed revision continuation has a real running/failed/stopped lifecycle execution but its local DEVELOPMENT record is missing, the same path can recover only that execution's validated Thread through `recoveryExecutionRunId`. `sessions:save` can update content but rejects phase or thread identity drift. Current inspection merges ProductPlan pages with TechnicalPlan references and projects API plus entity outlines (including field summaries/design status) for the workbench; page/API design status comes from formal planning artifacts rather than chat-session existence. | Read with `Frontend/src/main/stageSessions.ts` and `planningArtifactStatus.ts` when changing Electron lifecycle/IPC, stage-session allocation, template download, planning inspection, endpoint design-document status, app/session storage, preview, or filesystem-backed behavior. |
| `Frontend/src/main/stageSessions.ts`                                                                          | Current three-stage session primitives: product/planning/development mapping, per-Workflow/Stage sequence allocation, and an in-process serialized creation lock. It does not assign sessions to workflow/page/API/entity artifacts, create sessions for test/review/acceptance, or react to top-bar browsing. | Read with main-process `sessions:create`, renderer `service/chatSessions.ts`, and `AiChatPanel/hooks/useChatSessions.ts` when changing StageSession identity, sequence, or creation idempotency. |
| `Frontend/src/main/planningArtifactStatus.ts`                                                                 | Filesystem-backed endpoint design status: resolves the current `.devagentstudio/plans/endpoints/endpoint--<contractId>--<endpointId>.md` path and reports designed only for a non-empty Markdown document. | Read when changing workbench API design status or endpoint document naming. |
| `Frontend/src/main/filesystem.ts`                                                                            | Small Electron main-process filesystem helpers for idempotent `lstat`/recursive directory removal: an absent path is a successful no-op, while permission, type, symlink, and other filesystem errors remain visible to the caller. | Read when changing safe project deletion or other main-process filesystem operations that need explicit `ENOENT` semantics. |
| `Frontend/src/main/applicationSettings.ts`                                                                    | Versioned application-wide settings persisted atomically at Electron `app.getPath('userData')/settings.json`; currently owns the light/dark appearance theme, validates IPC writes, and broadcasts changes to every open Electron window. | Read when changing the application settings schema, theme persistence/defaults, settings IPC, or cross-window synchronization. |
| `Frontend/src/main/auth.ts`                                                                                   | Environment-scoped desktop authentication state: CMB Device Flow placeholder, current-process `auth.json` `{ access_token }` persistence, main-process memory access, and shared startup/reauthentication/quit cleanup. Cold starts delete any residual file before backend startup; ordinary window close only hides the window.                                                | Read when changing login, token persistence/memory semantics, environment auth paths, cold-start policy, or logout behavior.                                                            |
| `Frontend/src/main/backendService.ts`                                                                         | Packaged Windows/macOS backend service lifecycle: locating the PyInstaller executable, checking the generic built-in-skills resource directory, selecting a local port, health polling, renderer base URL, and process shutdown. Backend Python remains the authority for required skill names and files.        | Read when changing bundled backend startup, packaged resource structure, ports, health checks, or quit cleanup.                                                                         |

| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/utils/`                                                                            | Class name helper, layout helpers, preview URL/open helpers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Read for shared renderer utilities.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/styles/global.less` and component `.less` files                                    | Global and component-scoped styling.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Keep style changes near the component unless truly global.                                                                                                                             |
| `Frontend/scripts/verify-backend-resource.mjs`                                                                | Generic preflight check used by Windows/macOS Electron package scripts to ensure platform-specific `resources/backend/<platform>` contains the staged PyInstaller backend, `.env`, `_internal`, and `builtin_skills` directory. It intentionally does not duplicate Backend skill manifests.                       | Read when changing frontend desktop packaging or packaged resource structure.                                                                                                           |

## Generated Or External-Like Areas

| Path                                                        | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Frontend/node_modules/`, `Frontend/out/`, `Frontend/dist/` | Generated/dependency output; do not inspect for normal feature work.                                                                                                                                           |
| `Frontend/resources/backend/`                               | Generated platform backend staging output from `scripts/build-backend-win.ps1` and `scripts/build-backend-mac.sh`; contains PyInstaller support files and copied `.env`. Do not commit or inspect secrets.     |
| `Backend/build/`, `Backend/dist/`                           | Generated PyInstaller build output.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Backend/.venv/`, `Backend/.pycache/`                       | Environment/cache output; do not inspect for normal feature work.                                                                                                                                              |
| `var/workspaces/`                                           | Runtime workflow workspace artifacts such as generated specs, plans, JSON `build-task-plan.json` / `repair-task-plan.json`, reports, previews, and other local run output. Default workflow writes resolve here from the repository root. Do not treat as source unless a task explicitly asks to inspect a run artifact. |
| `.pnpm-store/`                                              | Package store; do not inspect.                                                                                                                                                                                 |

## Common Change Routes

| Task                                                   | Start here                                             | Usually also touch                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Add or change backend API route                        | `Backend/app/main.py`                                  | Runtime/tool module, frontend service caller, validation docs if behavior changes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Add or change workspace/file/git/terminal tool         | `Backend/app/workspace/workspace.py`                   | `Backend/app/main.py`, `Backend/app/tools/*` when exposed to DeepAgents, affected `Backend/app/agents/*`, frontend tool UI/service if exposed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Change app-generation workflow phase order/routing     | `docs/WORKFLOW.md`, `Backend/app/graph/workflow.py`    | `Backend/app/graph/state.py`, matching `graph/nodes/*`, `graph/subgraphs/unit_testing.py`, `graph/subgraphs/testing.py`, `graph/subgraphs/acceptance.py`, `protocols/workflow/`, `protocols/workflow_visualization.py`, frontend `workbenchPhase.ts`, `WorkbenchTopBar.tsx`, `MessageList`, `WorkflowRunCard`, `AiChatPanel` acceptance dock, and workflow debug UI when node selection changes. The current workbench order is design → development (Build → unit_test → unit_test_repair) → test (integration/repair/review confirmation) → review (code review → acceptance phase confirmation) → acceptance (launch → acceptance review → finalize), with separate unit-test/test-phase/review/acceptance confirmations and independent development/test/review/acceptance sessions. |
| Change workflow state or persisted artifact references | `Backend/app/graph/state.py`                           | Matching node, `workspace/*_documents.py`, `domain/models.py`, `workflow.md` if design semantics change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Change requirement confirmation in workflow            | `Backend/app/graph/nodes/requirements.py`              | `agents/main/requirements_analyzer.py`, `services/requirement_spec.py`, `services/entity_definitions.py`, `tools/ask_user.py`, and `workspace/spec_documents.py`. Requirement entities are display-only and never choose data sources; later product/technical/entity stages own those decisions. |
| Change product/technical planning or Endpoint API design/readiness | `Backend/app/graph/nodes/product_planning.py`, `Backend/app/graph/nodes/planning.py`, `Backend/app/graph/nodes/api_design.py` | ProductPlan owns visible behavior, UiManifest owns controls/local effects, TechnicalPlan owns API/schema, Endpoint identity, and Contract `entity_ids`, while Endpoint API Design owns each Endpoint's `fieldMappings`, table-first `databaseWrites`, query tree, database/external source snapshots, and business descriptions. Legacy EntitySourceBinding remains independent and outside normal Build readiness. |

The application-entry SessionRuntime keeps only an in-memory DAG Planning registry keyed by workspace/application and current Workflow node; phase remains display/context metadata. `starting`, `running`, and `stopping` in `prepare_build_tasks` make other sessions readonly, while API Design, Unit Test, Review, Acceptance, ordinary Build, and other non-DAG executions do not form a cross-session DAG lock. The DAG owner keeps its current workflow controls, while other history sessions remain viewable but replace the composer with a read-only notice and disable new-session actions. Creation metadata is immutable; no session is migrated or inferred from titles/messages. Temporary chat remains an unpersisted overlay without a target. `workflowCardResult` stores a submitted, confirmed, or cancelled Workflow-card choice on its source assistant message after the AG-UI action succeeds, so history restores the outcome instead of leaving the card in a pending state. Formal revisions retain the original planning Graph thread only in technical `revisionContext`; DESIGN→PLAN uses a separate visible thread and source receipt, while PLAN→DEVELOPMENT reuses the source DEVELOPMENT session/thread when available or creates a local output session when history is absent, appends a static prerequisite-updated entry card after its existing history, and lets that same thread own the automatic continuation, scan, DAG, and execution output. Continuation authority comes from the current backend revision and token; missing original local history creates a development output session instead of blocking continuation; failed design/plan stage startup removes only an unentered phase session with no durable source receipt before returning to the source stage. Ordinary top-bar browsing creates nothing. Environment session directories use normalized absolute `workspaceRoot`; permanent managed-project deletion removes them with renderer caches, index-only removal preserves history, and deleting an unfinished design-stage plan moves its `.devagentstudio` data and session root to the OS trash. |
